# Taittirīya-Brāhmaṇa - Black Yajur-Veda \[San\]

## Book: 1

## Chapter: 1

*Paragraph: 1*  
*Verse: 1 {BS 1.1.1.1}*  
*brahma samdhattam tan me jinvatam /*  
*kṣatraṁ samdhattam tan me jinvatam /*  
*iṣaṁ samdhattam tām me jinvatam /*  
*ūrjaṁ samdhattam tām me jinvatam /*  
*rayiṁ samdhattam tām me jinvatam /*  
*puṣṭiṁ samdhattam tām me jinvatam /*  
*prajāṁ samdhattam tām me jinvatam /*  
*paśūn samdhattam tān me jinvatam /*  
*stuto ‘si janadhāḥ /*  
*devās tvā śukrapāḥ praṇayantu //*  
*Verse: 2 {BS 1.1.1.2}*  
*suvīrāḥ prajāḥ prajanayan parīhi /*  
*śukraḥ śukraśociṣā /*  
*stuto ‘si janadhāḥ /*  
*devās tvā manthipāḥ praṇayantu /*  
*suprajāḥ prajāḥ prajanayan parīhi /*  
*manthī manthiśociṣā /*  
*samjagmānau diva ā pṛthivyāyuḥ /*  
*samdhattam tan me jinvatam /*  
*prāṇaṁ samdhattam tam me jinvatam /*  
*apānaṁ samdhattam tam me jinvatam //*  
*Verse: 3 {BS 1.1.1.3}*  
*vyānaṁ samdhattam tam me jinvatam /*  
*cakṣuḥ samdhattam tan me jinvatam /*  
*śrotraṁ samdhattam tan me jinvatam /*  
*manaḥ samdhattam tan me jinvatam /*  
*vācaṁ samdhattam tām me jinvatam /*  
*āyuḥ stha āyur me dhattam /*  
*āyur yajñāya dhattam /*  
*āyur yajñapataye dhattam /*  
*prāṇaḥ sthaḥ prāṇam me dhattam /*  
*prāṇam yajñāya dhattam //*  
*Verse: 4 {BS 1.1.1.4}*  
*prāṇam yajñapataye dhattam /*  
*cakṣuḥ sthaś {BS cakṣu sthaś} cakṣur me dhattam /*  
*cakṣur yajñāya dhattam /*  
*cakṣur yajñapataye dhattam /*  
*śrotraṁ sthaḥ śrotram me dhattam /*  
*śrotram yajñāya dhattam /*  
*śrotram yajñapataye dhattam /*  
*tau devau śukrāmanthinau /*  
*kalpayatam daivīr viśaḥ /*  
*kalpayatam mānuṣīḥ //*  
*Verse: 5 {BS 1.1.1.5}*  
*iṣam ūrjam asmāsu dhattam /*  
*prāṇān paśuṣu /*  
*prajām mayi ca yajamāne ca /*  
*nirastaḥ śaṇḍaḥ /*  
*nirasto markaḥ /*  
*apanuttau śaṇḍām arkau sahāmunā /*  
*śukrasya samid asi /*  
*manthinaḥ samid asi /*  
*sa prathamaḥ samkṛtir {AS samkṛtir} viśvakarmā /*  
*sa prathamo mitro varuṇo agniḥ /*  
*sa prathamo bṛhaspatiś cikitvān /*  
*tasmā indrāya sutam ājuhomi //*  
*Paragraph: 2*  
*Verse: 1 {BS 1.1.2.6}*  
*kṛttikāsv agnim ādadhīta /*

*agninakṣatram ity apacāyanti /*  
*gṛhān ha dāhuko bhavati /*  
*prajāpatī rohiṇyām agnim asṛjata /*

*ṛdhnoty eva /*

*teṣām anāhito ‘gnir āsīt /*

*te punarvasvor ādadhata /*

*uddhanti /*

*tad apahanti /*  
*apo ‘vokṣati śāntyai /*  
*sikatā nivapati /*

*abadhiro bhavati /*

*katham idaṁ syād iti /*

*asti vai tat /*

*{BS 1.1.4.34}*  
*bhṛgūṇām tvā̮aṅgirasām vratapate vratena̮ādadhāmīti bhṛgvaṅgirasām ādadhyāt /*  
*ādityānām tvā devānām vratapate vratena̮ādadhāmīty anyāsām brāhmaṇīnām prajānām /*

*indrasya tvā̮indriyeṇa vratapate vratena̮ādadhāmi̮iti rājanyasya /*  
*manos tvā grāmaṇyo vratapate vratena̮ādadhāmīti vaiśyasya /*  
*ṛbhūṇām tvā devānām vratapate vratena̮ādadhāmi̮iti rathakārasya /*

*vasīyān bhavati //*

*Verse: 1 {BS 1.1.5.35}*

*bhūr ity āha /*

*na̮enam pratinudante /*

*Paragraph: 10*  
*Verse: 1 {BS 1.1.10.75}*

## Chapter: 2

*Paragraph: 1*  
*Verse: 1 {BS 1.2.1.1}*  
*uddhanyamānam asyā amedhyam {BS amedhyam} /*  
*apa pāpmānam yajamānasya hantu /*  
*śivā naḥ santu pradiśaś catasraḥ /*  
*śam no mātā pṛthivī tokasātā /*

*āpo bhavantu pītaye /*  
*śam yor abhi sravantu naḥ /*  
*vaiśvānarasya rūpam /*  
*pṛthivyām parisrasā /*  
*syonam āviśantu {BS āviśantu} naḥ //*  
*Verse: 2 {BS 1.2.1.2}*  
*yad idam divo yad adaḥ pṛthivyāḥ /*  
*samjajñāne rodasī sambabhūvatuḥ /*  
*ūṣān kṛṣṇam avatu kṛṣṇam ūṣāḥ /*  
*iha̮ubhayor yajñiyam āgamiṣṭhāḥ /*  
*ūtīḥ kurvāṇo yat pṛthivīm acaraḥ /*

*śātam jīvema śaradaḥ savīrāḥ /*  
*ūrjam pṛthivyā rasam ābharantaḥ /*  
*śatam jīvema śaradaḥ purūcīḥ //*  
*Verse: 3 {BS 1.2.1.3}*  
*vamrībhir anuvittam guhāsu /*  
*śrotram ta urvy abadhirā bhavāmaḥ /*  
*prajāpatisṛṣṭānām prajānām /*

*upaprabhinnam iṣam ūrjam prajābhyaḥ /*  
*sūdam gṛhebhyo rasam ābharāmi /*

*guhā praviṣṭāṁ sarirasya madhye /*  
*tasyedam vihatam ābharantaḥ /*

*yat paryapaśyat sarirasya madhye /*

*atiprayacchan {BS ati prayaccham} duritim tareyam /*

## Chapter: 3

*Paragraph: 1*

*Verse: 1 {BS 1.5.12.58}*  
*tasya̮avāco {AS tasyā vāco} ‘vapādād abibhayuḥ /*

*yad aśrayan /*  
*tat̮śrāyantīyasya śrāyantīyatvam /*  
*yad avārayan /*  
*tad vāravantīyasya vāravantīyatvam /*  
*tasy̮avāca {AS tasyā vāca} eva̮avapādād abibhayuḥ /*  
*tasmā etāni sapta caturuttarāṇi chandāṁsy upādadhuḥ /*  
*teṣām ati trīṇy aricyanta /*  
*na trīṇy udabhavan //*  
*Verse: 2 {BS 1.5.12.59}*  
*sa bṛhatīm eva̮aspṛśat /*  
*dvābhyām akṣarābhyām /*  
*ahorātrābhyām eva /*  
*tad āhuḥ /*  
*katamā sā devākṣarā bṛhatī /*  
*yasyām tat pratyatiṣṭhat /*  
*dvādaśa paurṇamāsyaḥ /*  
*dvādaśa̮aṣṭakāḥ /*  
*dvādaśa̮amāvāsyāḥ /*  
*eṣā vāva sā devākṣarā bṛhatī //*  
*Verse: 3 {BS 1.5.12.60}*  
*yasyām tat pratyatiṣṭhad iti /*  
*yāni ca chandāṁsy atyaricyanta /*  
*yāni ca na̮udabhavan /*  
*tāni nirvīryāṇi hīnāny amanyanta /*  
*sā̮abravīd bṛhatī /*  
*mām eva bhūtvā /*  
*mām upa saṁśrayata̮iti /*  
*caturbhir akṣarair anuṣṭug bṛhatīm na̮udabhavat /*  
*caturbhir akṣaraiḥ paṅktir bṛhatīm atyaricyata /*  
*tasyām etāni catvāry akṣarāṇy apacchidya̮ādadhāt //*  
*Verse: 4 {BS 1.5.12.61}*  
*te bṛhatī eva bhūtvā /*  
*bṛhatīm upa samaśrayatām /*  
*aṣṭābhir akṣarair uṣṇig bṛhatīm na̮udabhavat /*  
*aṣṭābhir akṣarais triṣṭug bṛhatīm atyaricyata /*  
*tasyām etāny aṣṭāv akṣarāṇy apacchidya̮ādadhāt /*  
*te bṛhatī eva bhūtvā /*  
*bṛhatīm upa samaśrayatām /*  
*dvādaśabhir akṣarair gāyatrī bṛhatīm na̮udabhavat /*  
*dvādaśabhir akṣarair jagatī bṛhatīm atyaricyata /*  
*tasyām etāni dvādaśa̮akṣarāṇy apacchidya̮ādadhāt //*  
*Verse: 5 {BS 1.5.12.62}*  
*te bṛhatī eva bhūtvā /*  
*bṛhatīm upa samaśrayatām /*  
*so ‘bravīt prajāpatiḥ /*  
*chandāṁsi ratho me bhavata /*  
*yuṣmābhir aham etam adhvānam anu samcarāṇi̮iti /*  
*tasya gāyatrī ca jagatī ca pakṣāv abhavatām /*  
*uṣṇik ca triṣṭup ca praṣṭyau /*  
*anuṣṭup ca paṅktiś ca dhuryau /*  
*bṛhaty ṝva̮uddhir abhavat /*  
*sa etam chandoratham āsthāya /*  
*etam adhvānam anu samacarat /*  
*etaṁ ha vai chandoratham āsthāya /*  
*etam adhvānam anu samcarati /*  
*yenaiṣa etat samcarati /*  
*ya evam vidvān somena yajate /*  
*ya u cainam evam veda //*

## Chapter: 6

*Paragraph: 1*  
*Verse: 1 {BS 1.6.1.1}*  
*anumatyai puroḍāśam aṣṭākapālam nirvapati /*  
*ye pratyañcaḥ śamyāyā avaśīyante /*  
*tan nairṛtam ekakapālam /*  
*iyam vā anumatiḥ /*  
*iyam nirṛtiḥ /*  
*nairṛtena pūrveṇa pracarati /*  
*pāpmānam eva nirṛtim pūrvām niravadayate /*  
*ekakapālo bhavati /*  
*ekadhā̮eva nirṛtim niravadayate /*  
*yad ahutvā gārhapatya īyuḥ //*  
*Verse: 2 {BS 1.6.1.2}*  
*rudro bhūtvā̮agnir anūtthāya /*  
*adhvaryum ca yajamānam ca hanyāt /*

*na̮ārtim ārcchaty adhvaryur na yajamānaḥ /*

*eṣā vai nirṛtyai dik /*  
*svāyām eva diśi nirṛtim niravadayate //*  
*Verse: 3 {BS 1.6.1.3}*  
*svakṛta iriṇe juhoti pradare vā /*  
*etad vai nirṛtyā āyatanam /*  
*sva eva̮āyatane nirṛtim niravadayate /*

*nirdiśaty evainām /*  
*bhūte haviṣmaty asi̮ity āha /*  
*bhūtim eva̮upāvartate /*  
*muñca̮imam aṁhasa ity āha /*  
*aṁhasa evainam muñcati /*  
*aṅguṣṭhābhyām juhoti //*  
*Verse: 4 {BS 1.6.1.4}*  
*antata eva nirṛtim niravadayate /*  
*kṛṣṇam vāsaḥ kṛṣṇatūṣam dakṣiṇā /*

*rūpeṇa̮eva nirṛtim niravadayate /*

*nirṛtyā antarhityai /*

*ānumatena pracarati /*  
*iyam vā anumatiḥ //*  
*Verse: 5 {BS 1.6.1.5}*  
*iyam evāsmai rājyam anumanyate /*  
*dhenur dakṣiṇā /*  
*imām eva dhenum kurute /*  
*ādityam carum nirvapati /*  
*ubhayīṣv eva prajāsv abhiṣicyate /*  
*daivīṣu ca mānuṣīṣu ca /*  
*varo dakṣiṇā /*  
*varo hi rājyaṁ samṛddhyai /*  
*āgnāvaiṣṇavam ekādaśakapālam nirvapati /*  
*agniḥ sarvā devatāḥ //*  
*Verse: 6 {BS 1.6.1.6}*  
*viṣṇur yajñaḥ /*  
*devatāś caiva yajñam ca̮avarundhe /*  
*vāmano vahī dakṣiṇā /*  
*yad vahī /*  
*tena̮āgneyaḥ /*  
*yad vāmanaḥ /*  
*tena vaiṣṇavaḥ samṛddhyai /*

*Verse: 7 {BS 1.6.1.7}*  
*vārtraghnam eva vijityai /*  
*hiraṇyam dakṣiṇā samṛddhyai /*

*ṛṣabho vahī dakṣiṇā //*

*yad vahī /*  
*tena̮āgneyaḥ /*  
*yad ṛṣabhaḥ /*

*yad aindram dadhi //*  
*Verse: 9 {BS 1.6.1.9}*  
*indriyam eva̮avarundhe /*

*tena̮āgneyaḥ /*  
*yad ṛṣabhaḥ /*  
*tena̮aindraḥ samṛddhyai /*

*Verse: 10 {BS 1.6.1.10}*  
*devā vā oṣadhīṣv ājim ayuḥ /*

*aṣṭāv etāni havīṁṣi bhavanti /*  
*aṣṭākṣarā gāyatrī /*  
*gāyatro ‘gniḥ /*  
*tenaiva yajñamukhād ṛddhyā agner devatāyai na̮eti //*  
*Paragraph: 2*  
*Verse: 1 {BS 1.6.2.12}*  
*vaiśvadevena vai prajāpatiḥ prajā asṛjata /*  
*tāḥ sṛṣṭa na prājāyanta /*  
*so ‘gnir akāmayata /*  
*aham imāḥ prajanayeyam iti /*  
*sa prajāpataye /*  
*śucam adadhāt /*  
*so ‘śocat prajām icchamānaḥ /*  
*tasmād yam ca prajā bhunakti yam ca na /*  
*tāv ubhau śocataḥ prajām icchamānau /*  
*tāsv agnim apyasṛjat /*  
*tā agnir adhyait //*  
*Verse: 2 {BS 1.6.2.13}*  
*somo reto ‘dadhāt /*  
*savitā prājanayat /*  
*sarasvatī vācam adadhāt /*  
*pūṣā̮apoṣayat /*

*samvatsareṇa̮evāsmai prajāḥ prājanayat /*

*asmān api na prāyukṣata̮iti //*  
*Verse: 3 {BS 1.6.2.14}*  
*sa etam prajāpatir mārutaṁ saptakapālam apaśyat /*

*saptagaṇā vai marutaḥ /*  
*gaṇaśa evāsmai viśam kalpayati /*  
*sa prajāpatir aśocat //*  
*Verse: 4 {BS 1.6.2.15}*  
*yāḥ pūrvāḥ prajā asṛkṣi /*

*katham aparāḥ sṛjeyā̮iti /*

*tat prājāyata /*

*yad āmikṣā /*  
*yad vyuddharati //*

*prajā eva tad yajamānaḥ poṣayati /*

*so ‘gnir abravīt //*

*mām agre yajata /*  
*mayā mukhena̮asurāñ jeṣyatha̮iti /*

*mayā pratiṣṭhayā jeṣyatha̮iti //*

*tṝ ‘gninā mukhena̮asurān ajayan /*  
*somena rājñā /*  
*savitrā prasūtāḥ /*

*pūṣā pratiṣṭhā̮āsīt /*

*na̮uttaravedim upavapati /*  
*paśavo vā uttaravediḥ /*  
*ajātā iva hy ṝtarhi paśavaḥ //*  
*Paragraph: 3*  
*Verse: 1 {BS 1.6.3.19}*  
*trivṛd barhir bhavati /*  
*mātā pitā putraḥ /*  
*tad eva tan mithunam /*

*asminn eva tena loke pratitiṣṭhati /*

*bahurūpā hi paśavaḥ samṛddhyai /*

*yajamānasya̮avadyet /*  
*ud vā madyed yajamānaḥ /*

*yad ekakapālam āhavanīye juhoti /*

*yat pratyak //*

*yad udaṅ /*  
*manuṣyalokam abhijayet /*

*paśavo vai vājinam /*

*Paragraph: 4*  
*Verse: 1 {BS 1.6.4.29}*  
*prajāpatiḥ savitā bhūtvā prajā asṛjata /*  
*tā enam atyamanyanta /*  
*tā asmād apākrāman /*  
*tā varuṇo bhūtvā prajā varuṇena̮agrāhayat /*  
*tāḥ prajā varuṇagṛhītāḥ /*  
*prajāpatim punar upādhāvan nātham icchamānāḥ /*  
*sa etān prajāpatir varuṇapraghāsān apaśyat /*  
*tān niravapat /*  
*tair vai sa prajā varuṇapāśād amuñcat /*  
*yad varuṇapraghāsā nirupyante //*  
*Verse: 2 {BS 1.6.4.30}*  
*prajānām avaruṇagrāhāya /*  
*tāsām dakṣiṇo bāhur nyakna āsīt /*  
*savyaḥ prasṛtaḥ /*  
*sa etām dvitīyām dakṣiṇato vedim udahan /*  
*tato vai sa prajānām dakṣiṇam bāhum prāsārayat /*  
*yad dvitīyām dakṣiṇato vedim uddhanti /*  
*tasmāc cāturmāsyayājy amuṣmiṁl loka ubhayābāhuḥ /*  
*yajña̮abhijitaṁ hy asya /*  
*pṛthamātrād vedī asambhinne bhavataḥ //*  
*Verse: 3 {BS 1.6.4.31}*  
*tasmāt pṛthamātram vyaṁsau /*  
*uttarasyām vedyām uttaravedim upavapati /*  
*paśavo vā uttaravediḥ /*  
*paśūn eva̮avarundhe /*

## Chapter: 7

*Paragraph: 1*  
*Verse: 1 {BS 1.7.1.1}*  
*etad brāhmaṇāny eva pañca havīṁṣi /*

## Chapter: 2

*Paragraph: 1*  
*Verse: 1 {BS 2.2.1.1}*  
*prajāpatir akāmayata prajāḥ sṛjeya̮iti /*  
*sa etam daśahotāram apaśyat /*  
*tam manasā̮anudrutya darbhastambe ‘juhot /*  
*tato vai sa prajā asṛjata /*  
*tā asmāt sṛṣṭā apākrāman /*

*tad grahasya grahatvam /*

*sa daśahotāram manasā̮anudrutya darbhastambe juhuyāt /*  
*prajāpatir vai daśahotā //*  
*Verse: 2 {BS 2.2.1.2}*  
*prajāpatir eva bhūtvā prajāyate //*  
*manasā juhoti /*  
*mana iva hi prajāpatiḥ /*  
*prajāpater āptyai {//} pūrṇayā juhoti /*  
*pūrṇa iva hi prajāpatiḥ /*  
*prajāpaterāptyai //*  
*nyūnayā juhoti /*  
*nyūnād dhi prajāpatiḥ prajā asṛjata /*  
*prajānāṁ sṛṣṭyai //*  
*Verse: 3 {BS 2.2.1.3}*  
*darbhastambe juhoti /*  
*etasmād vai yoneḥ prajāpatiḥ prajā asṛjata /*

*tasmād eva yoneḥ pra jāyate //*  
*brāhmaṇo dakṣiṇata upāste /*  
*brāhmaṇo vai prajānām upadraṣṭā /*  
*upadraṣṭumaty eva prajāyate //*  
*graho bhavati /*

## Chapter: 5

*Paragraph: 1*

*{a} aśvinā havir indriyam /*

## Chapter: 7

*Paragraph: 1*

## Chapter: 8

*Paragraph: 1*  
*Verse: 1 {BS 2.8.1.1}*  
*{a} pīvonnāṁ {Dumont pīvoannāṁ} rayivṛdhaḥ sumedhāḥ /*  
*śvetaḥ siṣakti niyutām abhiśrīḥ /*  
*te vāyave samanaso vitasthuḥ /*  
*viśvā̮it̮ naraḥ svapatyāni cakruḥ /*  
*{b} rāye ‘nu yam jajñatū rodasī ubhe /*  
*rāye devī dhiṣaṇā dhāti devam /*  
*adhā vāyum niyutaḥ saścata svāḥ /*  
*uta śvetam vasudhitim nireke /*  
*{c} ā vāyo, {d} pra yābhiḥ /*  
*{d} pra vāyum acchā bṛhatī manīṣā //*  
*Verse: 2 {BS 2.8.1.2}*  
*bṛhadrayim viśvavārāṁ {Dumont viśvavāraṁ} rathaprām /*  
*dyutadyāmā niyutaḥ patyamānaḥ /*  
*kaviḥ kavim iyakṣasi prayajyo /*  
*{f} ā no niyudbhiḥ śatinībhir adhvaram /*  
*sahasriṇībhir upa yāhi yajñam /*  
*vāyo asmin haviṣi mādayasva /*  
*yūyam pāta svastibhiḥ sadā naḥ /*  
*{g} prajāpate na tvad etāny anyaḥ /*  
*viśvā jātāni pari tā babhūva /*  
*yatkāmās te juhumas tan no astu //*  
*Verse: 3 {BS 2.8.1.3}*  
*vayaṁ syāma patayo rayīṇām /*  
*{h} rayīṇām patim yajatam bṛhantam /*  
*asmin bhare nṛtamam vājasātau /*  
*prajāpatim prathamajām ṛtasya /*  
*yajāma devam adhi no bravītu /*  
*{i} prajāpate tvam nidhipāḥ purāṇaḥ /*  
*devānām pitā janitā prajānām /*  
*patir viśvasya jagataḥ paraspāḥ /*  
*havir no deva vihave juṣasva /*  
*{j} tava̮ime lokāḥ pradiśo diśaś ca //*  
*Verse: 4 {BS 2.8.1.4}*  
*parāvato nivata udvataś ca /*  
*prajāpate viśvasṛj jīvadhanya idam no deva /*  
*pratiharya havyam /*  
*{k} prajāpatim prathamam yajñiyānām /*

*sa no dadātu draviṇaṁ {AS draviṇaṁ} suvīryam /*  
*rāyas poṣam viṣyatu nābhim asme /*  
*{l} yo rāya īśe śatadāya ukthyaḥ /*  
*yaḥ paśūnāṁ rakṣitā viṣṭhitānām /*

*agnir vā akāmayata /*

*prajāpatiḥ prajā asṛjata /*

*sa etam ajāya̮ekapade proṣṭhapadebhyaś carum niravapat /*

*tejasvī ha vai brahmavarcasī bhavati /*  
*ya etena haviṣā yajate /*  
*ya u ca̮enad evam veda /*  
*so ‘tra juhoti /*  
*ajāya̮ekapade svāhā proṣṭhapadebhyaḥ svāhā /*  
*tejase svāhā brahmavarcasāya svāhā̮iti //*  
*Verse: 11 {BS 3.1.5.52}*  
*ahir vai budhniyo ‘kāmayata /*  
*imām pratiṣṭhām vindeya̮iti /*  
*sa etam ahaye budhniyāya proṣṭhapadebhyaḥ puroḍāśam bhūmikapālam niravapat /*  
*tato vai sa imām pratiṣṭhām avindata /*  
*imāṁ ha vai pratiṣṭhām vindate /*  
*ya etena haviṣā yajate /*  
*ya u ca̮enad evam veda /*  
*so ‘tra juhoti /*  
*ahaye budhniyāya svāhā proṣṭhapadebhyaḥ svāhā /*  
*pratiṣṭhāyai svāhā̮iti //*  
*Verse: 12 {BS 3.1.5.53}*  
*pūṣā vā akāmayata /*  
*paśumānt syām iti /*  
*sa etam pūṣṇe revatyai carum niravapat /*  
*tato vai sa paśumān abhavat /*  
*paśumān ha vai bhavati /*  
*ya etena haviṣā yajate /*  
*ya u ca̮enad evam veda /*  
*so ‘tra juhoti /*  
*pūṣṇe svāhā revatyai svāhā /*  
*paśubhyaḥ svāhā̮iti //*  
*Verse: 13 {BS 3.1.5.54}*  
*aśvinau vā akāmayetām /*  
*śrotrasvināv abadhirau syāva̮iti /*  
*tav etam aśvibhyām aśvayugbhyām puroḍāśam dvikapālam niravapatām /*  
*tato vai tau śrotrasvināv abadhirāv abhavatām /*  
*śrotrasvī ha vā abadhiro bhavati /*  
*ya etena haviṣā yajate /*  
*ya u ca̮enad evam veda /*  
*so ‘tra juhoti /*  
*aśvibhyāṁ svāhā̮aśvayugbhyāṁ svāhā /*  
*śrotrāya svāhā śrutyai svāhā̮iti //*  
*Verse: 14 {BS 3.1.5.55}*  
*yamo vā akāmayata /*  
*pitṛṇāṁ rājyam abhijayeyam iti /*  
*sa etam yamāyā̮apabharaṇībhyaś carum niravapat /*  
*tato vai sa pitṛṇāṁ rājyam abhyajayat /*  
*samānānāṁ ha vai rājyam abhijayati /*  
*ya etena haviṣā yajate /*  
*ya u ca̮enad evam veda /*  
*so ‘tra juhoti /*  
*yamāya svāhā̮apabharaṇībhyaḥ svāhā /*  
*rājyāya svāhā̮abhijityai svāhā̮iti //*  
*Verse: 15 {BS 3.1.5.56}*  
*athaitad amāvāsyāyā ājyam nirvapati /*  
*kāmo vā amāvāsyā /*  
*kāma ājyam /*  
*kāmena̮eva kāmaṁ samardhayati /*  
*kṣipram enaṁ sa kāma upanamati /*  
*yena kāmena yajate /*  
*so ‘tra juhoti /*  
*amāvāsyāyai svāhā kāmāya svāhā̮āgatyai svāhā̮iti //*  
*Paragraph: 6*  
*Verse: 1 {BS 3.1.6.57}*  
*candramā vā akāmayata /*  
*ahorātrān ardhamāsān māsān ṛtūnt samvatsaram āptvā /*  
*candramasaḥ sāyujyaṁ salokatām āpnuyām iti /*  
*sa etam candramase pratīdṛśyāyai puroḍāśam pañcadaśakapālam niravapat /*  
*tato vai so ‘horātrān ardhamāsān māsān ṛtūnt samvatsaram āptvā /*  
*candramasaḥ sāyujyaṁ salokatām āpnot /*  
*ahorātrān ha vā ardhamāsān māsān ṛtūnt samvatsaram āptvā /*  
*candramasaḥ sāyujyaṁ salokatām āpnoti /*  
*ya etena haviṣā yajate /*  
*ya u ca̮enad evam veda /*  
*so ‘tra juhoti /*  
*candramase svāhā pratīdṛśyāyai svāhā /*  
*ahorātrebhyaḥ svāhā̮ardhamāsebhyaḥ svāhā /*  
*māsebhyaḥ svāhā̮ṛtubhyaḥ svāhā /*  
*samvatsarāya svāhā̮iti //*  
*Verse: 2 {BS 3.1.6.58}*  
*ahorātre vā akāmayetām /*  
*aty ahorātre mucyevahi /*  
*na nāv ahorātre āpnuyātām iti /*  
*te etam ahorātrābhyām carum niravapatām /*  
*dvayānām vrīhīṇām /*  
*śuklānām ca kṛṣṇānām ca /*  
*savātyor dugdhe /*  
*śvetāyai ca kṛṣṇāyai ca /*  
*tato vai te aty ahorātre amucyete /*  
*na̮ene ahorātre āpnutām /*  
*ati ha vā ahorātre mucyate /*  
*na̮enam ahorātre āpnutaḥ /*  
*ya etena haviṣā yajate /*  
*ya u ca̮enad evam veda /*  
*so ‘tra juhoti /*  
*ahne svāhā rātriyai svāhā /*  
*atimuktyai svāhā̮iti //*  
*Verse: 3 {BS 3.1.6.59}*  
*uṣā vā akāmayata /*  
*priyā̮ādityasya subhagā syām iti /*  
*sa̮etam uṣase carum niravapat /*  
*tato vai sā priyā̮ādityasya subhagā̮abhavat /*  
*priyo ha vai samānānāṁ subhago bhavati /*  
*ya etena haviṣā yajate /*  
*ya u ca̮enad evam veda /*  
*so ‘tra juhoti /*  
*uṣase svāhā vyḥṣṭyai svāhā /*  
*vyūṣuṣyai svāhā vyḥcchantyai svāhā /*  
*vyḥṣṭāyai svāhā̮iti //*  
*Verse: 4 {BS 3.1.6.60}*  
*atha̮etasmai nakṣatrāya carum nirvapati /*  
*yathā tvam devānām asi /*  
*evam aham manuṣyāṇām bhūyāsam iti /*  
*yathā ha vā etad devānām /*  
*evaṁ ha vā eṣa manuṣyāṇām bhavati /*  
*ya etena haviṣā yajate /*  
*ya u ca̮enad evam veda /*  
*so ‘tra juhoti /*  
*nakṣatrāya svāhā̮udeṣyate svāhā /*  
*udyate svāhā̮uditāya svāhā /*  
*harase svāhā bharase svāhā /*  
*bhrājase svāhā tejase svāhā /*  
*tapase svāhā brahmavarcasāya svāhā̮iti //*  
*Verse: 5 {BS 3.1.6.61}*  
*sūryo vā akāmayata /*  
*nakṣatrāṇām pratiṣṭhā syām iti /*  
*sa etaṁ sūryāya nakṣatrebhyaś carum niravapat /*  
*tato vai sa nakṣatrāṇām pratiṣṭhā̮abhavat /*  
*pratiṣṭhā ha vai samānānām bhavati /*  
*ya etena haviṣā yajate /*  
*ya u ca̮enad evam veda /*  
*so ‘tra juhoti /*  
*sūryāya svāhā nakṣatrebhyaḥ svāhā /*  
*pratiṣṭhāyai svāhā̮iti //*  
*Verse: 6 {BS 3.1.6.62}*  
*atha̮etam adityai carum nirvapati /*  
*iyam vā aditiḥ /*  
*asyām eva pratitiṣṭhati /*  
*so ‘tra juhoti /*  
*adityai svāhā pratiṣṭhāyai svāhā̮iti //*  
*Verse: 7 {BS 3.1.6.63}*  
*atha̮etam viṣṇave carum nirvapati /*  
*yajño vai viṣṇuḥ /*  
*yajña eva̮antataḥ pratitiṣṭhati /*  
*so ‘tra juhoti /*  
*viṣṇave svāhā yajñāya svāhā /*  
*pratiṣṭhāyai svāhā̮iti //*

## Chapter: 2

*Paragraph: 1*  
*Verse: 1 {BS 3.2.1.1}*  
*{a} tṛtīyasyām ito divi soma āsīt /*  
*tam gāyatry āharat /*  
*tasya parṇam acchidyata /*  
*tat parṇo ‘bhavat /*  
*tat parṇasya parṇatvam /*  
*brahma vai parṇaḥ /*  
*yat parṇaśākhayā vatsān apākaroti /*  
*brahmaṇā̮evainān apākaroti /*  
*gāyatro vai parṇaḥ /*  
*gāyatrāḥ paśavaḥ //*  
*Verse: 2 {BS 3.2.1.2}*  
*tasmāt trīṇi̮trīṇi parṇasya palāśāni /*  
*tripadā gāyatrī /*  
*yat parṇaśakhayā gāḥ prārpayati /*  
*svayā̮evainā devatayā prārpayati /*  
*{b} yam kāmayeta̮apaśuḥ syād iti /*  
*aparṇām tasmai śuṣkāgrām āharet /*  
*apaśur eva bhavati /*  
*yam kāmayeta paśumānt syād iti /*  
*bahuparṇām tasmai bahuśākhām āharet /*  
*paśumantam evainam karoti //*  
*Verse: 3 {BS 3.2.1.3}*  
*yat prācīm āharet /*  
*devalokam abhijayet /*  
*yad udīcīm manuṣyalokam /*  
*prācīm udīcīm āharati /*  
*ubhayor lokayor abhijityai /*  
*{c} iṣe tvā̮ūrje tvā̮ity āha /*  
*iṣam eva̮ūrjam yajamāne dadhāti /*  
*vāyavaḥ stha̮ity āha /*  
*vāyur vā antarikṣasya̮adhyakṣāḥ /*  
*antarikṣadevatyāḥ khalu vai paśavaḥ //*  
*Verse: 4 {BS 3.2.1.4}*  
*vāyava evainān paridadāti /*  
*pra vā enān etad ākaroti /*  
*yad āha /*  
*vāyavaḥ stha̮ity upāyavaḥ stha̮ity āha /*  
*yajamānāya̮eva paśūn upahvayate /*  
*devo vaḥ savitā prārpayatv ity āha prasūtyai /*  
*śreṣṭhatamāya karmaṇa ity āha /*  
*yajño hi śreṣṭhatamam karma /*  
*tasmād evam āha /*  
*{d} āpyāyadhvam aghniyā devabhāgam ity āha //*  
*Verse: 5 {BS 3.2.1.5}*  
*vatsebhyaś ca vā etāḥ purā mauṣyṝbhyaś ca̮āpyāyanta /*  
*devebhya evainā indrāya̮āpyāyati /*  
*ūrjasvatīḥ payasvatīr ity āha /*  
*ūrjaṁ hi payaḥ sambharanti /*  
*prajāvatīr anamīvā ayakṣmā ity āha prajātyai /*  
*mā vaḥ stena {AS va stena} īśata mā̮aghaśaṁsa ity āha guptyai /*  
*rudrasya hetiḥ pari vo vṛṇaktv ity āha /*  
*rudrād evainās trāyate /*  
*dhruvā asmin gopatau syāta bahvīr ity āha /*  
*dhruvā evāsmin bahvīḥ karoti //*  
*Verse: 6 {BS 3.2.1.6}*  
*yajamānasya paśūn pāhi̮ity āha /*  
*paśunām gopīthāya /*  
*tasmāt sāyam paśava upa samāvartante /*  
*{e} anadhaḥ sādayati /*  
*garbhāṇām dhṛtyā aprapādāya /*  
*tasmād garbhāḥ prajānām aprapādukāḥ /*  
*upari̮iva nidadhāti /*  
*upari̮iva hi suvargo lokaḥ /*  
*suvargasya lokasya samaṣṭyai //*  
*Paragraph: 2*  
*Verse: 1 {BS 3.2.2.7}*  
*{a} devasya tvā savituḥ prasava ity aśvaparśum ādatte prasūtyai /*  
*aśvinor bāhubhyām ity āha /*  
*aśvinau hi devānām adhvaryū āstām /*  
*pūṣṇo hastābhyām ity āha yatyai /*  
*yo vā oṣadhīḥ parvaśo veda /*  
*na̮enāḥ sa hinasti /*  
*prajāpatir vā oṣadhīḥ parvaśo veda /*  
*sa enā na hinasti aśvaparśvā barhir acchaiti /*  
*prājāpatyo vā aśvaḥ sayonitvāya //*  
*Verse: 2 {BS 3.2.2.8}*  
*oṣadhīnām ahiṁsāyai /*  
*{b} yajñasya ghoṣad asi̮ity āha /*  
*yajamāna eva rayim dadhāti /*  
*pratyuṣṭaṁ rakṣaḥ pratyuṣṭā arātaya ity āha /*  
*rakṣasām apahatyai /*  
*pra̮iyam agād dhiṣaṇā barhir acchā̮ity āha /*  
*vidyā vai dhiṣaṇā /*  
*vidyayā̮evainad acchaiti /*  
*manunā kṛtā svadhayā vitaṣṭa̮ity āha /*  
*mānavī hi parśuḥ svadhākṛtā //*  
*Verse: 3 {BS 3.2.2.9}*  
*{c} ta āvahanti kavayaḥ purastād ity āha /*  
*śuśruvāṁso vai kavayaḥ /*  
*yajñaḥ purastāt /*  
*mukhata eva yajñam ārabhate /*  
*atho yad etad uktvā yataḥ kutaś ca̮āharati /*  
*tat prācyā eva diśo bhavati /*  
*devebhyo juṣṭam iha barhir āsada ity āha /*  
*barhiṣaḥ samṛddhyai /*  
*karmaṇo ‘naparādhāya /*  
*devānām pariṣūtam asi̮ity āha //*  
*Verse: 4 {BS 3.2.2.10}*  
*yad vā idam kim ca /*  
*tad devānām pariṣūtam /*  
*atho yathā vasyase pratiprocya̮āha̮idam kariṣyāmi̮iti /*  
*evam eva tad adhvaryur devebhyaḥ pratiprocya barhir dāti /*  
*ātmano ‘hiṁsāyai /*  
*{d} yāvataḥ stambān paridiśet /*  
*yat teṣām ucchiṁṣyāt /*  
*ati tad yajñasya recayet /*  
*ekaṁ ṁ stambam paridiśet /*  
*taṁ sarvam dāyāt //*  
*Verse: 5 {BS 3.2.2.11}*  
*yajñasya̮anatirekāya /*  
*varṣavṛddham asi̮ity āha /*  
*varṣavṛddhā vā oṣadhayaḥ /*  
*devabarhir ity āha /*  
*devebhya eva̮enat karoti /*  
*mā tvā̮anvaṅ mā tiryag ity āha̮ahiṁsāyai /*  
*parva te rādhyāsam ity āha̮ṛdhyai /*  
*ācchettā te mā riṣam ity āha /*  
*na̮asya̮ātmano mīyate /*  
*ya evam veda //*  
*Verse: 6 {BS 3.2.2.12}*  
*deva barhiś śatavalśam viroha̮ity āha /*  
*prajā vai barhiḥ /*  
*prajānām prajananāya /*  
*sahasravalśā vi vayaṁ ruhema̮ity āha /*  
*āśiṣam eva̮etām āśāste /*  
*pṛthivyāḥ sampṛcaḥ pāhi̮ity āha pratiṣṭhityai /*  
*ayum gāyum gān {Dumont ayuṅgāyuṅgān} muṣṭīn̮lunoti /*  
*mithunatvāya prajātyai /*  
*{f} susambhṛtā tvā sambharāmi̮ity āha /*  
*brahmaṇā̮evainat sambharati //*  
*Verse: 7 {BS 3.2.2.13}*  
*adityai rāsnā̮asi̮ity āha /*  
*iyam vā aditiḥ /*  
*asyā evainad rāsnām karoti /*  
*indrāṇyai samnahanam ity āha /*  
*indrāṇī vā agre devatānāṁ samanahyata /*  
*sā̮ardhnot /*  
*ṛddhyai samnahyati /*  
*prajā vai barhiḥ /*  
*prajānām aparāvāpāya /*  
*tasmāt snāvasamtatāḥ prajā jāyante*  
*Verse: 8 {BS 3.2.2.14}*  
*{g} pūṣā te granthim grathnātv ity āha /*  
*puṣṭim eva yajamāne dadhāti /*  
*sa te mā sthād ity āha̮ahiṁsāyai /paścāt prāñcam upagūhati /*  
*paścād vai prācīnaṁ reto dhīyate /*  
*paścād evāsmai prācīnaṁ reto dadhāti /*  
*indrasya tvā bāhubhyām udyaccha ity āha /*  
*indriyam eva yajamāne dadhāti /*  
*bṛhaspater mūrdhnā harāmi̮ity āha /*  
*brahma vai devānām bṛhaspatiḥ //*  
*Verse: 9 {BS 3.2.2.15}*  
*brahmaṇā̮evainad dharati /*  
*urv antarikṣam anvihītyāha gatyai /*  
*devamgamam asīty āha /*  
*devān eva̮enad gamayati /*  
*{h} anadhaḥ sādayati /*  
*garbhāṇām dhṛtyā aprapādāya /*  
*tasmād garbhāḥ prajānām aprapādukāḥ /*  
*uparīva nidadhāti /*  
*uparīva hi suvargo lokaḥ /*  
*suvargasya lokasya samaṣṭyai //*  
*Paragraph: 3*  
*Verse: 1 {BS 3.2.3.16}*  
*pūrvedyur idhmābarhiḥ karoti /*  
*yajñam eva̮ārabhya gṛhītvā̮upavasati /*  
*prajāpatir yajñam asṛjata /*  
*tasya̮ukhe asraṁsetām /*  
*yajño vai prajāpatiḥ /*  
*yat sāmnāyya̮ukhe bhavataḥ /*  
*yajñasya̮eva tad ukhe upadadhāty aprasraṁsāya /*  
*{b} śundhadhvam daivyāya karmaṇe devayajyāyā ity āha /*  
*devayajyāyā eva̮enāni śundhati /*  
*mātariśvano gharmo ‘si̮ity āha //*  
*Verse: 2 {BS 3.2.3.17}*  
*antarikṣam vai mātariśvano gharmaḥ /*  
*eṣām lokānam vidhṛtyai /*  
*dyaur asi pṛthivy asīty āha /*  
*divaś ca hy ṝṣā pṛthivyāś ca sambhṛtā /*  
*yad ukhā /*  
*tasmād evam āha /*  
*viśvadhāyā asi parameṇa dhāmnā̮ity āha /*  
*vṛṣṭir vai viśvadhāyāḥ /*  
*vṛṣṭim eva̮avarundhe /*  
*dṛṁhasva mā hvār ity āha dhṛtyai //*  
*Verse: 3 {BS 3.2.3.18}*  
*{c} vasūnām pavitram asīty āha /*  
*prāṇā vai vasavaḥ /*  
*teṣām vā etad bhāgadheyam /*  
*yat pavitram /*  
*tebhya eva̮enat karoti /*  
*śatadhāraṁ sahasradhāram ity āha /*  
*prāṇeṣv evāyur dadhāti sarvatvāya /*  
*trivṛt palāśaśākhāyām darbhamayam bhavati /*  
*trivṛd vai prāṇaḥ /*  
*trivṛtam eva prāṇam madhyato yajamāne dadhāti //*  
*Verse: 4 {BS 3.2.3.19}*  
*saumyaḥ parṇaḥ sayonitvāya /*  
*sākṣāt pavitram darbhāḥ /*  
*{d} prāk sāyam adhinidadhāti /*  
*tat prāṇāpānayo rūpam /*  
*tiryak prātaḥ /*  
*tad darśasya rūpam /*  
*dārśyaṁ hy ṝtad ahaḥ /*  
*annam vai candramāḥ /*  
*annam prāṇāḥ /*  
*ubhayam eva̮upaity ajāmitvāya //*  
*Verse: 5 {BS 3.2.3.20}*  
*tasmād ayaṁ sarvataḥ pavate /*  
*{e} hutaḥ stoko huto drapsa ity āha pratiṣṭhityai /*  
*haviśo ‘skandāya /*  
*na hi hutaṁ svāhākṛtaṁ skandati /*  
*divi nāko nāmāgniḥ /*  
*tasya vipruṣo bhāgadheyam /*  
*agnaye bṛhate nākāya̮ity āha /*  
*nākam evāgnim bhāgadheyena samardhayati /*  
*svāhā dyāvāpṛthivībhyām ity āha /*  
*dyāvāpṛthivyor eva̮enat pratiṣṭhāpayati //*  
*Verse: 6 {BS 3.2.3.21}*  
*pavitravaty ānayati /*  
*apām ca̮eva̮oṣadhīnām ca rasaṁ saṁsṛjati /*  
*atho oṣadhīṣv eva paśūn pratiṣṭhāpayati /*  
*{f} anvārabhya vācam yacchati /*  
*yajñasya dhṛtyai /*  
*dhārayann āste /*  
*dhārayanta iva hi duhanti /*  
*kām adhukṣa ity āha̮ā tṛtīyasyai /*  
*traya ime lokāḥ /*  
*imān eva lokān yajamāno duhe //*  
*Verse: 7 {BS 3.2.3.22}*  
*amūm iti nāma gṛhṇāti /*  
*bhadram evāsām karmāviṣkaroti /*  
*sā viśvāyuḥ sā viśvavyacāḥ sā viśvakarma̮ity āha /*  
*iyam vai viśvāyuḥ /*  
*antarikṣam viśvavyacāḥ /*  
*asau viśvakarmā /*  
*imān eva̮etābhir lokān yathāpūrvam duhe /*

*āpo vai rakṣoghnīḥ //*

*sphyam udasyan /*

*śucā̮evainam arpayati //*

*Verse: 1 {BS 3.2.10.83}*  
*{a} vajro vai sphyaḥ /*

*vajre ‘dhvaryuḥ kṣaṇvīta /*

*agnibhyām prācaś ca pratīcaś ca /*  
*sphyena̮udīcaś ca̮adhrācaś ca /*

*utkare ‘dhi pravṛścati //*

*yathā̮upadhāya vṛścanty evam /*  
*{b} hastāv avanenikte /*

*yajñasya mithunatvāya /*  
*atho purorucam evaitām dadhāti /*  
*uttarasya karmaṇo ‘nukhyātyai /*  
*{c} na purastāt pratyag upasādayet //*  
*Verse: 3 {BS 3.2.10.85}*  
*yat purastāt pratyag upasādayet /*  
*anyatra̮āhutipathād idhmam pratipādayet /*

*sampraty ṝva barhiṣā prajānām prajananam upaiti /*

*uttaram barhiḥ /*  
*ātmā vā idhmaḥ /*  
*prajā barhiḥ /*

*tato medham upanīya /*

## Chapter: 3

*Paragraph: 1*  
*Verse: 1 {BS 3.3.1.1}*  
*{a} pratyuṣṭaṁ rakṣaḥ pratyuṣṭā arātaya ity āha /*  
*rakṣasām apahatyai /*  
*agner vas tejiṣṭhena tejasā niṣṭapāmīty āha medhyatvāya /*  
*srucaḥ sammārṣṭi /*  
*sruvam agre /*  
*pumāṁsam eva̮abhyaḥ saṁśyati mithunatvāya /*  
*atha juhūm /*  
*atha̮upabhṛtam /*  
*atha dhruvām /*  
*asau vai juhūḥ //*  
*Verse: 2 {BS 3.3.1.2}*  
*antarikṣam upabhṛt /*  
*pṛthivī dhruvā /*  
*ime vai lokāḥ srucaḥ /*  
*vṛṣṭiḥ sammārjanāni /*  
*vṛṣṭir vā imān̮ lokān anupūrvam kalpayati /*  
*te tataḥ kḷptāḥ samedhante /*  
*samedhante ‘smā ime lokāḥ prajayā paśubhiḥ /*  
*ya evam veda /*  
*{b} yadi kāmayeta varṣukaḥ parjanyaḥ syād iti /*  
*agrataḥ sammṛjyāt //*  
*Verse: 3 {BS 3.3.1.3}*  
*vṛṣṭim eva niyacchati /*  
*arvācīnāgrā {BS avācīnāgrā} hi vṛṣṭiḥ /*  
*yadi kāmayetāvarṣukaḥ syād iti /*  
*mūlataḥ sammṛjyāt /*  
*vṛṣṭim eva̮udyacchati /*  
*tad u vā āhuḥ /*  
*agrata eva̮upariṣṭāt sammṛjyāt /*  
*mūlato ‘dhastāt /*  
*tad anupūrvam kalpate /*  
*varṣuko bhavatīti //*  
*Verse: 4 {BS 3.3.1.4}*  
*{c} prācīm abhyākāram /*  
*agrair antarataḥ /*  
*evam iva hy annam adyate /*  
*atho agrād vā oṣadhīnām ūrjam prajā upajīvanti /*  
*ūrja eva̮annādyasyāvarudhyai {AS ̮āvaruddhyai} /*  
*adhastāt pratīcīm /*  
*daṇḍam uttamataḥ /*  
*mūlena mūlam pratiṣṭhityai /*  
*tasmād aratnau prāñcy upariṣṭāl lomāni /*  
*pratyañcy adhastāt //*  
*Verse: 5 {BS 3.3.1.5}*  
*srug ghy ṝṣā /*  
*{d} prāṇo vai sruvaḥ /*  
*juhūr dakṣiṇo hastaḥ /*  
*upabhṛt savyaḥ /*  
*ātmā dhruvā /*  
*annaṁ sammārjanāni /*  
*mukhato vai prāṇo ‘pāno bhūtvā /*

*prajāḥ prajanayan /*

*Paragraph: 11*  
*Verse: 1 {BS 3.3.11.76}*  
*{a} pariveṣo vā eṣa vanaspatīnām /*  
*yad upaveṣaḥ /*  
*ya evam veda /*  
*vindate pariveṣṭāram /*  
*tam utkare /*  
*yam devā manuṣyṝṣu /*  
*upaveṣam adhārayan /*  
*ye asmad apacetasaḥ /*  
*tān asmabhyam iha̮ākuru /*  
*upaveṣa̮upaviḍḍhi naḥ //*  
*Verse: 2 {BS 3.3.11.77}*  
*prajām puṣṭim atho dhanam /*  
*dvipado naś catuṣpadaḥ /*

*tasmāt purastāt pratyañcaḥ śūdrā avasyanti /*  
*sthavimata upagūhati /*  
*aprativādina evainān kurute /*  
*{b} dhṛṣṭir vā upaveṣaḥ /*  
*śucā̮ṛto vajro brahmaṇā saṁśitaḥ /*  
*yā̮upaveṣe śuk /*  
*sāmum ṛcchatu yam dviṣma iti //*  
*Verse: 3 {BS 3.3.11.78}*  
*athāsmai nāmagṛhya praharati /*  
*nir amum nuda okasaḥ /*  
*sapatno yaḥ pṛtanyati /*  
*nirbādhyṝna haviṣā /*  
*indra eṇam parāśarīt /*  
*ihi tisraḥ parāvataḥ /*  
*ihi pañca janāṁ ati /*  
*ihi tisro ‘ti rocanā, yāvat /*  
*sūryo asad divi /*  
*paramām tvā parāvatam //*  
*Verse: 4 {BS 3.3.11.79}*  
*indro nayatu vṛtrahā /*  
*yato na punar āyasi /*  
*śaśvatībhyaḥ samābhya iti /*  
*{c} trivṛd vā eṣa vajro brahmaṇā saṁśitaḥ /*  
*śucā̮evainam viddhvā /*

## Chapter: 6

*Paragraph: 1*  
*Verse: 1 {BS 3.6.1.1}*  
*{a} añjanti tvām adhvare devayantaḥ /*  
*vanaspate {AS /} madhunā {BI madhunā} daivyena /*  
*yad ūrdhvas tiṣṭhād {Dumont tiṣṭhā} draviṇā̮iha dhattāt /*  
*yad vā kṣayo mātur asyā upasthe /*  
*{b} ucchrayasva vanaspate /*  
*varṣaman pṛthivyā adhi /*  
*sumitī mīyamānaḥ /*  
*varco dhā yajñavāhase /*  
*{c} samiddhasya śrayamāṇaḥ purastāt /*  
*brahma vanvāno ajaraṁ suvīram //*  
*Verse: 2 {BS 3.6.1.2}*  
*āre asmad amatim bādhamānaḥ /*  
*ucchrayasva mahate saubhagāya /*  
*{d} ūrdhva ū ṣu ṇa ūtaye /*  
*tiṣṭhā devo na savitā /*  
*ūrdhvo vājasya sanitā yad añjibhiḥ /*  
*vāghadbhir vihvayāmahe /*  
*{e} ūrdhvo naḥ pāhy aṁhaso niketunā /*  
*viśvaṁ sam attriṇam daha /*  
*kṛdhī na ūrdhvān {BS ūrdhvāñ} ca rathāya {Dumont carathāya} jīvase /*  
*vidā deveṣu no duvaḥ //*  
*Verse: 3 {BS 3.6.1.3}*  
*{f} jāto jāyate sudinatve ahnām /*  
*sa marya {Dumont samarya} ā vidathe vardhamānaḥ /*  
*punanti dhīrā apaso manīṣā /*  
*devayā vipra udiryati vācam /*  
*{g} yuvā suvāsāḥ parivīta āgāt /*  
*sa u śreyān bhavati jāyamānaḥ /*  
*tam dhīrāsaḥ kavaya unnayanti /*  
*svādhiyo manasā devayantaḥ /*  
*{h} pṛthupājā amartyaḥ /*  
*ghṛtanirṇik svāhutaḥ /*  
*{BS 3.6.1.4}*  
*agnir yajñasya havyavāṭ /*  
*{i} taṁ sabādho yatasrucaḥ {BS yatasrḥcaḥ} /*  
*itthā dhiyā yajñavantaḥ /*  
*ācakrur agnim ūtaye /*  
*{j} tvam varuṇa uta mitro agne /*  
*tvām vardhanti matibhir vasiṣṭhāḥ /*  
*tve vasu suṣaṇanāni santu /*  
*yūyam pāta svastibhiḥ sadā naḥ //*  
*Paragraph: 2*  
*Verse: 1 {BS 3.6.2.5}*  
*{a} hotā yakṣad agniṁ samidhā suṣamidhā samiddham nābhā pṛthivyāḥ samgathe vāmasya /*  
*varṣman diva iḍas pade vetv ājyasya hotar yaja /*  
*{b} hotā yakṣat tanūnapātam aditer garbham bhuvanasya gopām /*  
*madhvā̮adya devo devebhyo devayānān patho anaktu vetv ājyasya hotar yaja /*  
*{c} hotā yakṣan narāśaṁsam nṛśastram nṝṁḥpraṇetram /*  
*gobhir vapāvānt syād vīraiḥ śaktīvān rathaiḥ prathamayāvā hiraṇyaiś candrī vetv ājyasya hotar yaja /*  
*{d} hotā yakṣad agnim iḍa īḍito devo devāṁ āvakṣad dūto havyavāḍ amūraḥ /*  
*upa̮imam yajñam upa̮imām devo devahūtim avatu vetv ājyasya hotar yaja /*  
*{e} hotā yakṣad barhiḥ suṣṭarīma̮ūrṇamradā asmin yajñe vi ca pra ca prathatāṁ svāsastham devebhyaḥ /*  
*ā̮īm enad adya vasavo rudrā ādityāḥ sadantu priyam indrasyāstu vetv ājyasya hotar yaja //*  
*Verse: 2 {BS 3.6.2.6}*  
*{f} hotā yakṣad dura ṛṣvāḥ kavaṣyo ‘koṣadhāvanīr ud ātābhir jihatām vi pakṣobhiḥ śrayantām /*  
*suprāyaṇā asmin yajñe viśrayantām ṛtāvṛdho viyantv ājyasya hotar yaja /*  
*{g} hotā yakṣad uṣāsānaktā bṛhatī supeśasā nṝṁḥpatibhyo yonim kṛṇvāne /*  
*saṁsmayamāne indreṇa devairā̮idam barhiḥ sīdatām vītām ājyasya hotar yaja /*  
*{h} hotā yakṣad daivyā hotārā mandrā potārā kavī pracetasā /*  
*svṅṣṭam adyānyaḥ karad iṣā svabhigūrtam anya ūrjā satavasā̮imam yajñam divi deveṣu dhattām vītām ājyasya hotar yaja /*  
*Verse: 3 {BS 3.6.2.6}*  
*{i} hotā yakṣat tisro devīr apasām apastamā acchidram adya̮idam apas tanvatām /*  
*devebhyo devīr devam apo viyantv ājyasya hotar yaja /*  
*{j} hotā yakṣat tvaṣṭāram aciṣṭum apākaṁ retodhām viśravasam yaśodhām /*  
*pururūpam akāmakarśanaṁ supoṣaḥ poṣaiḥ syāt suvīro vīrair vetv ājyasya hotar yaja /*  
*{k} hotā yakṣad vanaspatim upāvasrakṣad dhiyo joṣṭāraṁ śaśaman naraḥ /*  
*svadāt svadhitir ṛtuthādya devo devebhyo havyāvāḍ vetv ājyasya hotar yaja /*  
*{l} hotā yakṣad agniṁ svāhā̮ājyasya svāhā medasaḥ svāhā stokānāṁ svāhā svāhākṛtīnāṁ svāhā havyasūktīnām /*  
*svāhā devāṁ ājyapānt svāhā̮agniṁ hotrāj juṣāṇā agna ajyasya viyantu hotar yaja //*  
*Paragraph: 3*  
*Verse: 1 {BS 3.6.3.7}*  
*{a} samiddho adya manuṣo duroṇe /*  
*devo devān yajasi jātavedaḥ /*  
*ā ca vaha mitramahaś cikitvān /*  
*tvam dūtaḥ kavir asi pracetāḥ /*  
*{b} tanūnapāt patha ṛtasya yānān /*  
*madhvā samañjant svadayā sujihva /*  
*manmāni dhībhir uta yajñam ṛndhan /*  
*devatrā ca kṛṇuhy adhvaram naḥ /*  
*{c} narāśaṁsasya mahimānameṣām /*  
*upastoṣāma yajatasya yajñaiḥ //*  
*Verse: 2 {BS 3.6.3.8}*  
*te sukratavaḥ śucayo dhiyamdhāḥ /*  
*svadantu devā ubhayāni havyā /*  
*{d} ājuhvāna īḍyo vandyaś ca /*  
*āyāhy agne vasubhiḥ sajoṣāḥ /*  
*tvam devānām asi yahva hotā /*  
*sa enān yakṣīṣito yajīyān /*  
*{e} prācīnam barhiḥ pradiśā pṛthivyāḥ /*  
*vastor asyā vṛjyate agre ahnām /*  
*vy ḥ prathate vitaram varīyaḥ /*  
*devebhyo aditaye syonam //*  
*Verse: 3 {BS 3.6.3.9}*  
*{f} vyacasvatīr urviyā viśrayantām /*  
*patibhyo na janayaḥ śumbhamānāḥ /*  
*devīr dvāro bṛhatīr viśvaminvāḥ /*  
*devebhyo bhavatha suprāyaṇāḥ /*  
*{g} ā suṣvayantī yajate upāke /*  
*uṣāsānaktā sadatām ni yonau /*  
*divye yoṣaṇe bṛhatī surukme /*  
*adhi śriyaṁ śukrapiśam dadhāne /*  
*{h} daivyā hotārā prathamā suvācā /*  
*mimānā yajñam manuṣo yajadhyai //*  
*Verse: 4 {BS 3.6.3.10}*  
*pracodayantā vidatheṣu kārū /*  
*prācīnam jyotiḥ pradiśā diśantā /*  
*{i} ā no yajñam bhāratī tūyam etu /*  
*iḍā manuṣvad iha cetayantī /*  
*tisro devīr barhir ā̮idaṁ syonam /*  
*sarasvatīḥ svapasaḥ sadantu /*  
*{j} ya ime dyāvāpṛthivī janitrī /*  
*rūpair apiṁśad bhuvanāni viśvā /*  
*tam adya hotar iṣito yajīyān /*  
*devam tvaṣṭāram iha yakṣi vidvān //*  
*Verse: 5 {BS 3.6.3.11}*  
*{k} upāvasṛjat tmanyā samañjan /*  
*devānām pātha ṛtuthā havīṁṣi /*  
*vanaspatiḥ śamitā devo agniḥ /*  
*svadantu havyam madhunā ghṛtena /*  
*{l} sadyo jāto vyamimīta yajñam /*  
*agnir devānām abhavat purogāḥ /*  
*asya hotuḥ pradiśy ṛtasya vāci /*  
*svāhākṛtaṁ havir adantu devāḥ //*  
*Paragraph: 4*  
*Verse: 1 {BS 3.6.4.12}*  
*{a} agnir hotā no adhvare /*

*ā deveṣu prayo dadhat /*

*Verse: 1 {BS 3.6.5.13}*  
*ajaid agniḥ /*

*devo devebhyo havyā vāṭ /*

*Paragraph: 6*  
*Verse: 1 {BS 3.6.6.14}*  
*{a} daivyāḥ śamitāra uta manuṣyā ārabhadhvam /*

*āśāsānā medhapatibhyām medham /*  
*{b} prāsmā agnim bharata /*  
*stṛṇīta barhiḥ /*  
*anv enam mātā manyatām /*  
*anu pitā /*

*anu sakhā sayūthyaḥ /*

*Verse: 2 {BS 3.6.6.15}*

## Chapter: 10

*Paragraph: 1*  
*Verse: 1 {BS 3.10.1.1}*  
*{a} samjñānam vijñānam prajñānam jānad abhijānat /*  
*samkalpamānam prakalpamānam upakalpamānam upakḷptam kḷptam /*  
*śreyo vasīya āyat sambhūtam bhūtam /*  
*{b} citraḥ ketuḥ prabhān ābhānt sambhān /*  
*jyotiṣmāṁs tejasvān ātapaṁs tapann abhitapan /*  
*rocano rocamānaḥ śobhanaḥ śobhamānaḥ kalyāṇaḥ /*  
*{c} darśā dṛṣṭā darśatā viśvarūpā sudarśanā /*  
*āpyāyamānā pyāyamānā pyāyā sūnṛtā̮irā /*  
*āpūryamāṇā pūryamāṇā pūrayantī pūrṇā paurṇamāsī /*  
*{d} dātā pradātā̮ānando modaḥ pramodaḥ /*  
*Verse: 2 {BS 3.10.1.2}*  
*āveśayan niveśayant samveśanaḥ saṁśāntaḥ śāntaḥ /*  
*ābhavan prabhavant sambhavant sambhūto bhūtaḥ /*  
*{e} prastutam viṣṭutaṁ saṁstutam kalyāṇam viśvarūpam /*  
*śukram amṛtam tejasvi tejaḥ samiddham /*  
*aruṇam bhānuman marīcimad abhitapat tapasvat /*  
*{f} savitā prasavitā dīpto dīpayan dīpyamānaḥ /*  
*jvalañ jvalitā tapan vitapant samtapan /*  
*rocano rocamānaḥ śumbhūḥ śumbhamāno vāmaḥ /*  
*{g} sutā sunvatī prasutā sūyamānā̮abhiṣūyamāṇā /*  
*pītī prapā sampā tṛptis tarpayantī /*  
*Verse: 3 {BS 3.10.1.3}*  
*kāntā kāmyā kāmajātā̮āyuṣmatī kāmadughā /*  
*{h} abhiśāstā̮anumantā̮ānando modaḥ pramodaḥ /*  
*āsādayan niṣādayant saṁsādanaḥ saṁsannaḥ sannaḥ /*  
*ābhūr vibhūḥ prabhūḥ śambhūr bhuvaḥ /*  
*{i} pavitram paviyiṣyan pūto medhyaḥ /*  
*yaśo yaśasvān āyur amṛtaḥ /*  
*jīvo jīviṣyant svargo lokaḥ /*  
*{j} sahasvānt sahīyān ojasvānt sahamānaḥ /*  
*jayann abhijayant sudraviṇo draviṇodāḥ /*  
*ārdrapavitro harikeśo modaḥ pramodaḥ //*  
*Verse: 4 {BS 3.10.1.4}*  
*{k} aruṇo ‘ruṇarajāḥ puṇḍarīko viśvajid abhijit /*  
*ārdraḥ pinvamāno ‘nnavān rasavān irāvān /*  
*sarvauṣadhaḥ sambharo mahasvān /*  
*{l} ejatkā jovatkāḥ /*  
*kṣullakāḥ śipiviṣṭakāḥ /*  
*sarisrarāḥ suśeravaḥ /*  
*ajirāso gamiṣṇavaḥ /*  
*{m} idānīm tadānīm etarhi kṣipram ajiram /*  
*āśur nimeṣaḥ phaṇo dravann atidravan /*  
*tvaraṁs tvaramāṇa āśur āśīyāñ javaḥ {Dumont javaḥ \[?\]} /*  
*{n} agniṣṭoma ukthyo ‘tirātro dvirātras trirātraś catūrātraḥ /*  
*agnir ṛtuḥ sūrya ṛtuś candramā ṛtuḥ /*  
*{o} prajāpatiḥ samvatsaro mahān kaḥ //*  
*Paragraph: 2*  
*Verse: 1 {BS 3.10.2.5}*  
*bhūr agnim ca pṛthivīm ca mām ca /*  
*trīṁś ca lokānt samvatsaram ca /*  
*prajāpatis tvā sādayatu /*  
*tayā devatayā̮aṅgirasvad dhruvā sīda //*  
*bhuvo vāyum cāntarikṣam ca mām ca /*  
*trīṁśca lokānt samvatsaram ca /*  
*prajāpatis tvā sādayatu /*  
*tayā devatayā̮aṅgirasvad dhruvā sīda //*  
*svar ādityam ca divam ca mām ca /*  
*trīṁś ca lokānt samvatsaram ca /*  
*prajāpatis tvā sādayatu /*  
*tayā devatayā̮aṅgirasvad dhruvā sīda //*  
*bhūr bhuvaḥ svaś candramasam ca diśaś ca mām ca /*  
*trīṁś ca lokānt samvatsaram ca /*  
*prajāpatis tvā sādayatu /*  
*tayā devatayā̮aṅgirasvad dhruvā sīda //*

*tena me jvala /*  
*tena me dīdihi /*  
*yavad devāḥ /*  
*yāvad asāti sūryaḥ /*

*Verse: 1 {BS 3.10.5.10}*  
*bhūr bhuvaḥ svaḥ /*

*yaśo mahat /*

*vipaścite pavamānāya gāyata /*

*Paragraph: 9*  
*Verse: 1 {BS 3.10.9.23}*  
*{a} prajāpatir devān asṛjata /*  
*te pāpmanā samditā ajāyanta /*  
*tān vyadyat /*  
*yad vyadyat /*  
*tasmād vidyut /*  
*tam avṛścat /*  
*yad avṛścat /*  
*tasmād vṛṣṭiḥ /*  
*tasmād yatra̮ete devate abhiprāpnutaḥ {BS abhiyāpnutaḥ} /*  
*vi ca ha̮evāsya tatra pāpmānam dyataḥ /*  
*Verse: 2 {BS 3.10.9.24}*  
*vṛścataś ca //*  
*sā̮eṣā mīmāṁsā̮agnihotra eva sampannā {BS,AS sampannā} /*  
*atho āhuḥ /*  
*sarveṣu yajñakratuṣv iti //*  
*hoṣyann apa upaspṛśet /*  
*vidyud asi vidya me pāpmānam iti /*  
*atha hutvā̮upaspṛśet /*  
*vṛśṭir asi vṛśca me pāpmānam iti /*  
*yakṣyamāṇo vā̮iṣṭvā vā /*  
*vi ca ha̮evāsya̮ete devate pāpmānam dyataḥ /*  
*Verse: 3 {BS 3.10.9.24}*  
*vṛścataś ca //*  
*{b} atyaṁho ha̮āruṇiḥ /*  
*brahmacāriṇe praśnān procya prajighāya /*  
*parehi /*  
*plakṣam daiyāmpātim pṛccha /*  
*vettha sāvitrā3n {BS sāvitrā3m} na vetthā3 iti /*  
*tam āgatya papraccha /*  
*ācāryo mā prā ha̮eṣīt /*  
*vettha sāvitrā3n {BS sāvitrā3m} na vetthā3 iti /*  
*sa ha̮uvāca veda̮iti //*  
*Verse: 4 {BS 3.10.9.26}*  
*sa kasmin pratiṣṭhita iti /*  
*parorajasīti /*  
*kas tad yat parorajā iti /*  
*eṣa vāva sa parorajā iti ha̮uvāca /*  
*ya eṣa tapati /*  
*eṣo ‘rvāgrajā iti /*  
*sa kasmin tv eṣa iti /*  
*satya iti /*  
*kim tat satyam iti /*  
*tapa iti /*  
*Verse: 5 {BS 3.10.9.27}*  
*kasmin nu tapa iti /*  
*bala iti /*  
*kim tad balam iti /*  
*prāṇa iti /*  
*mā sma prāṇam atipṛccha iti mā̮ācāryo ‘bravīd iti ha̮uvāca brahmacārī //*  
*sa ha̮uvāca plakṣo daiyāmpātiḥ /*  
*yad vai brahmacārin prāṇam atyaprakṣyaḥ {BS,AS atyaprakṣyaḥ} /*  
*mūrdhā te vyapatiṣyat {BS,AS vyapatiṣyat} /*  
*aham u ta ācāryāt̮śreyān bhaviṣyāmi /*  
*yo mā sāvitre samavādiṣṭa̮iti /*  
*Verse: 6 {BS 3.10.9.28}*  
*tasmāt sāvitre na samvadeta //*  
*{c} sa yo ha vai sāvitram viduṣā sāvitre samvadate /*  
*sahāsmin̮śriyam dadhāti /*  
*anu ha vā asmā asau tapan̮śriyam manyate /*  
*anv asmai śrīs tapo manyate /*  
*anv asmai tapo balam manyate /*  
*anv asmai balam prāṇam manyate //*  
*{d} sa yad āha /*  
*samjñānam vijñānam darśā dṛṣṭā̮iti /*  
*eṣa eva tat /*  
*Verse: 7 {BS 3.10.9.29}*  
*atha yad āha /*  
*prastutam viṣṭutaṁ sutā sunvatīti /*  
*eṣa eva tat //*  
*eṣa hy ṝva tāny ahāni /*  
*eṣa rātrayaḥ //*  
*atha yad āha /*  
*citraḥ ketur dātā pradātā savitā prasavitā̮abhiśāstā̮anumantā̮iti /*  
*eṣa eva tat /*  
*eṣa hy ṝva te ‘hno muhūrtāḥ /*  
*eṣa rātreḥ //*  
*Verse: 8 {BS 3.10.9.30}*  
*atha yad āha /*  
*pavitram pavayiṣyant sahasvānt sahīyān aruṇo ‘ruṇarajā iti /*  
*eṣa eva tat /*  
*eṣa hy ṝva te ‘rdhamāsāḥ /*  
*eṣa māsāḥ //*  
*atha yad āha /*  
*agniṣṭoma ukthyo ‘gnir ṛtuḥ prajāpatiḥ samvatsara iti /*  
*eṣa eva tat /*  
*eṣa hy ṝva te yajñakratavaḥ /*  
*eṣa ṛtavaḥ /*  
*Verse: 9 {BS 3.10.9.31}*  
*eṣa samvatsaraḥ //*  
*atha yad āha /*  
*idānīm tadānīm iti /*  
*eṣa eva tat /*  
*eṣa hy ṝva te muhūrtānām {BS mudvūrtānām} muhūrtāḥ {BS muddūrtāḥ} /*  
*{e} janako ha vaidehaḥ /*  
*ahorātraiḥ samājagāma /*  
*taṁ ha̮ūcuḥ /*  
*yo vā asmān veda /*  
*vijahat pāpmānam eti /*  
*Verse: 10 {BS 3.10.9.32}*  
*sarvam āyur eti /*  
*abhi svargam lokam jayati /*  
*nāsyāmuṣmin̮loke ‘nnam kṣīyata iti //*  
*vijahad dha vai pāpmānam eti /*  
*sarvam āyur eti /*  
*abhi svargam lokam jayati /*  
*nāsyāmuṣmin̮loke ‘nnam kṣīyate /*  
*ya evam veda //*  
*ahīnā ha̮āśvatthyaḥ /*  
*sāvitram vidāmcakāra /*  
*Verse: 11 {BS 3.10.9.33}*  
*sa ha haṁso hiraṇmayo bhūtvā /*  
*svargam lokam iyāya /*  
*ādityasya sāyujyam //*  
*haṁso ha vai hiraṇmayo bhūtvā /*  
*svargam lokam eti /*  
*ādityasya sāyujyam /*  
*ya evam veda //*  
*devabhāgo ha śrautarṣaḥ /*  
*sāvitram vidāmcakāra /*  
*taṁ ha vāg adṛśyamānā̮abhyuvāca /*  
*Verse: 12 {BS 3.10.9.34}*  
*sarvam bata gautamo veda /*  
*yaḥ sāvitram veda̮iti /*  
*sa ha̮uvāca /*  
*kā̮eṣā vāg asīti /*  
*ayam ahaṁ sāvitraḥ /*  
*devānām uttamo lokaḥ /*  
*guhyam maho bibhrad iti /*  
*etāvati ha gautamaḥ /*  
*yajñopavītam kṛtvā̮adho nipapāta /*  
*namo nama iti /*  
*Verse: 13 {BS 3.10.9.35}*  
*sa ha̮uvāca /*  
*mā bhaiṣīr gautama /*  
*jito vai te loka iti //*  
*tasmād ye ke ca sāvitram viduḥ /*  
*sarve te jitalokāḥ //*  
*sa yo ha vai sāvitrasyāṣṭākṣaram padaṁ śriyā̮abhiṣiktam veda /*  
*śriyā ha̮evābhiṣicyate /*  
*ghṛṇir iti dve akṣare /*  
*sūrya iti trīṇi /*  
*āditya iti trīṇi /*  
*Verse: 14 {BS 3.10.9.36}*  
*etad vai sāvitrasyāṣṭākṣaram padaṁ śriyā̮abhiṣiktam /*  
*ya evam veda /*  
*śriyā haivā̮abhiṣicyate //*  
*tad etad ṛcā̮abhyuktam /*  
*ṛco akṣare parame vyoman /*  
*yasmin devā adhi viśve niṣeduḥ /*  
*yas tan na veda kim ṛcā kariṣyati /*  
*ya it tad vidus ta ime samāsata iti //*  
*na ha vā etasya̮ṛcā na yajuṣā na sāmnā̮artho ‘sti /*  
*yaḥ sāvitram veda //*

*tad etat pari yad devacakram /*  
*ārdram pinvamānaṁ svarge loka eti /*

*vijahan viśvā bhūtāni sampaśyan /*  
*ya evam veda //*  
*śūṣo ha vai vārṣṇeyaḥ /*  
*ādityena samājagāma /*  
*taṁ ha̮uvāca /*  
*ehi sāvitram viddhi /*  
*ayam vai svargyo ‘gniḥ pārayiṣṇur amṛtāt sambhūta iti /*  
*eṣa vāva sa sāvitraḥ /*  
*ya eṣa tapati /*  
*ehi mām viddhi /*  
*iti ha̮evainam tad uvāca //*  
*Paragraph: 10*  
*Verse: 1 {BS 3.10.10.38}*  
*iyam vāva saraghā /*

*tā madhukṛtaḥ /*  
*yāny ahāni /*  
*te madhuvṛṣāḥ /*  
*sa yo ha vā etā madhukṛtaś ca madhuvṛṣāṁś ca veda /*  
*kruvanti hāsya̮etā agnau madhu /*  
*nāsya̮iṣṭāpūrtam dhayanti /*  
*atha yo na veda /*  
*Verse: 2 {BS 3.10.10.39}*  
*na hāsya̮etā agnau madhu kurvanti /*

*nāhorātreṣv ārtim ārcchati //*  
*samjñānam vijñānam darśā dṛṣṭā̮iti /*

*prastutam viṣṭutaṁ sutā sunvatī̮iti /*  
*etāv anuvākāv aparapakṣasyāhorātrāṇām nāmadheyāni /*  
*nāhorātreṣv ārtim ārcchati /*  
*ya evam veda //*  
*Verse: 3 {BS 3.10.10.40}*  
*yo ha vai muhūrtānām nāmadheyāni veda /*  
*na muhūrteṣv ārtim ārcchati //*

*etṝ ‘nuvākā muhūrtānām nāmadheyāni /*  
*na muhūrteṣv ārtim ārcchati /*  
*ya evam veda //*

*etṝ ‘nuvākā ardhamāsānām ca māsānām ca nāmadheyāni /*  
*Verse: 4 {BS 3.10.10.41}*  
*nārdhamaseṣu na māseṣv ārtim ārcchati /*  
*ya evam veda //*  
*yo ha vai yajñakratūnām ca̮ṛtūnām ca samvatsarasya ca nāmadheyāni veda /*  
*na yajñakratuṣu na̮ṛtuṣu na samvatsara ārtim ārcchati //*  
*agniṣṭoma ukthyo ‘gnir ṛtuḥ prajāpatiḥ samvatsara iti /*  
*etṝ ‘nuvākā yajñakratūnām ca̮ṛtūnām ca samvatsarasya ca nāmadheyāni /*  
*na yajñakratuṣu na̮ṛtuṣu na samvatsara ārtim ārcchati /*  
*ya evam veda //*  
*yo ha vai muhūrtānām muhūrtān veda /*  
*na muhūrtānām muhūrteṣv ārtim ārcchati //*  
*Verse: 5 {BS 3.10.10.42}*  
*idānīm tadānīm iti /*  
*ete vai muhūrtānām muhūrtāḥ /*  
*na muhūrtānām muhūrteṣv ārtim ārcchati /*

*atho yathā kṣetrajño bhūtvā̮anupraviśyānnam atti /*  
*evam eva̮etān kṣetrajño bhūtvā̮anupraviśyānnam atti /*  
*sa eteṣām eva salokatāṁ sāyujyam aśnute /*  
*apa punarmṛtyum jayati /*  
*ya evam veda //*  
*Paragraph: 11*  
*Verse: 1 {BS 3.10.11.43}*  
*kaś cid dha vā asmāl lokāt pretya /*  
*ātmānam veda /*  
*āyam aham asmīti /*  
*kaś cit svam lokam na pratiprajānāti /*  
*agnimugdho ha̮eva dhumatāntaḥ /*  
*svam lokam na pratiprajānāti /*  
*atha yo ha̮evaitam agniṁ sāvitram veda /*  
*sa evāsmāl lokāt pretya /*  
*ātmānam veda /*  
*ayam aham asmīti /*  
*Verse: 2 {BS 3.10.11.44}*  
*sa svam lokam pratiprajānāti /*  
*eṣa u ca̮evainam {BS uvevainam} tat sāvitraḥ /*  
*svargam lokam abhivahati //*  
*ahorātrair vā idaṁ sayugbhiḥ kriyate /*  
*itirātrāyā̮adīkṣiṣata /*  
*itirātrāya vratam upāgur iti /*  
*tāni hānevam viduṣaḥ /*  
*amuṣmin̮ loke śevadhim dhayanti /*  
*dhītaṁ ha̮eva sa śevadhim anuparaiti /*  
*atha yo ha̮evaitam agniṁ sāvitram veda //*  
*Verse: 3 {BS 3.10.11.45}*  
*tasya ha̮evāhorātrāṇi /*  
*amuṣmin̮loke śevadhim na dhayanti /*  
*adhītaṁ ha̮eva sa śevadhim anu paraiti //*  
*bharadvājo ha tribhir āyurbhir brahmacaryam uvāsa /*  
*taṁ ha jīrṇiṁ sthaviraṁ śayānam /*  
*indra upavrajya̮uvāca /*  
*bharadvāja /*  
*yat te caturtham āyur dadyām /*  
*kim enena kuryā iti /*  
*brahmacaryam eva̮enena careyam iti ha̮uvāca /*  
*Verse: 4 {BS 3.10.11.46}*  
*taṁ ha trīn girirūpān avijñātān iva darśayām cakāra /*  
*teṣāṁ ha̮ekaikasmān muṣṭin ādade /*  
*sa ha̮uvāca /*  
*bharadvāja̮ity āmantrya /*  
*vedā vā ete /*  
*anantā vai vedāḥ /*  
*etad vā etais tribhir āyurbhir anvavocathāḥ /*  
*atha ta itarad ananūktam eva /*  
*ehīmam viddhi /*  
*ayam vai sarvavidyā̮iti //*  
*Verse: 5 {BS 3.10.11.47}*  
*tasmai ha̮etam agniṁ sāvitram uvāca /*  
*taṁ sa viditvā /*  
*amṛto bhūtvā /*  
*svargam lokam iyāya /*  
*ādityasya sāyujyam //*  
*amṛto ha̮eva bhūtvā /*  
*svargam lokam eti /*  
*ādityasya sāyujyam /*  
*ya evam veda //*  
*eṣo eva trayī vidyā /*  
*Verse: 6 {BS 3.10.11.48}*  
*yāvantaṁ ha vai trayyā vidyayā lokam jayati /*  
*tāvantam lokam jayati /*  
*ya evam veda //*  
*agner vā etāni nāmadheyāni /*  
*agner eva sāyujyaṁ salokatām āpnoti /*  
*ya evam veda /*  
*vāyor vā etāni nāmadheyāni /*  
*vāyor eva sāyujyaṁ salokatām āpnoti /*  
*ya evam veda /*  
*indrasya vā etāni nāmadheyāni /*  
*Verse: 7 {BS 3.10.11.49}*  
*indrasya̮eva sāyujyaṁ salokatām āpnoti /*  
*ya evam veda /*  
*bṛhaspater vā etāni nāmadheyāni /*  
*bṛhaspater eva sāyujyaṁ salokatām āpnoti /*  
*ya evam veda /*  
*prajāpater vā etāni nāmadheyāni /*  
*prajāpater eva sāyujyaṁ salokatām āpnoti /*  
*ya evam veda /*  
*brahmaṇo vā etāni nāmadheyāni /*  
*brahmaṇa eva sāyujyaṁ salokatām āpnoti /*  
*ya evam veda //*  
*sa vā eṣo ‘gnir apakṣapuccho vāyur eva /*  
*tasyāgnir mukham /*  
*asāv ādityaḥ siraḥ /*  
*sa yad ete devate antareṇa /*  
*tat sarvaṁ sīvyati /*  
*tasmāt sāvitraḥ //*
